## Best date plans

### 2026-09-12 — Evelina + Newlyweeds with Shaka King Q&A

**Dinner, then Newlyweeds with Shaka King Q&A.** Italian and Mediterranean at Evelina. It is 1.8 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. Newlyweeds with Shaka King Q&A starts at 7 pm, so book the table early.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=211+Dekalb+Ave%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11205&destination=Brooklyn+Ave+%26+Prospect+Pl%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11213&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://rooftopfilms.com/event/newlyweeds/) · [Restaurant](https://www.evelinabk.com/)

### 2026-09-13 — Pioneer Works Second Sundays + Winner

**Pioneer Works Second Sundays, then a meal.** Bakery and casual American at Winner. It is 1.8 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. It starts at 12 pm, which leaves the evening for the table.

#### [NYC Resistor Masks-On Public Craft Night](https://www.eventbrite.com/e/public-craft-night-1st-fridays-and-3rd-wednesdays-of-the-month-masks-on-tickets-623419182557)

6:30 PM · workshop · Free; RSVP appreciated · NYC Resistor · Gowanus · Brooklyn · Score 69.0  
A masks-required public craft night at NYC Resistor where attendees bring projects and work alongside artists, crafters, and makers.
**Timing:** First Fridays and third Wednesdays, 6:30-9:30pm.
**Practical note:** Fourth-floor makerspace; masks are required for this version of craft night.

## Where to eat

Verified against captured official menu/event evidence and limited follow-up public pages; corrected duplicate offers, ordinary-menu classifications, dated opening claims, and unsupported price/award language.

Dining research: 2026-09-11T10:38:56+00:00

No direct Michelin Guide page was available in the provided evidence. COTE's homepage makes a broad brand claim as America's first and only Michelin-starred Korean steakhouse, but that was not applied specifically to COTE 550. Eater's Michelin-starred taqueria language for El Califa de Leon refers to the Mexico City original in 2024, not the New York outpost.

Conditions: Oysters and clams have a minimum order of 6. Reservations link is provided. Official page states automatic 20% gratuity for large parties of 6 or more; tax/service charges otherwise not stated.

#### [64th New York Film Festival](https://www.filmlinc.org/nyff/)

2026-09-25 · Festival runs September 25-October 12, 2026; public tickets go on sale Tuesday, September 15 at noon ET. · film · Ticket prices vary by screening and pass · Film at Lincoln Center · Lincoln Square · Manhattan · Score 67.0  
The 64th New York Film Festival returns to Film at Lincoln Center with a day-by-day schedule and lineup available before the public on-sale.
**Why now:** Public ticket sales open September 15 for festival dates beyond the seven-day window.
**Hot booking alert:** NYFF64 tickets go on public sale September 15 at noon ET, with member and pass-holder presales before that; high-demand screenings can move quickly once public sales open.

2026-11-06 · On view November 6-December 19, 2026; opening reception November 5, 6-8pm. · art · Free · Luhring Augustine Chelsea · Chelsea · Manhattan · Score 58.0
